Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The petrochemical operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 868. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 650 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 650. QED.
